Errors emit error with exc_type, exc_msg, traceback.

4. New CLI flag: dataclaw status --logs tails the latest N lines of today's log and pretty-prints. dataclaw status --logs --run <id> filters to one run.

5. Tauri UI adds a 'Logs' tab (not just Findings): streams the current log file via a Rust watcher (notify crate) to the frontend, color-codes INFO/WARN/ERROR, has copy-to-clipboard. 'Show in Finder' button opens ~/.dataclaw/logs/.

6. launchd plist (scheduler step) already sets StandardOutPath/StandardErrorPath — keep that as coarse capture; the JSON log is the fine-grained one. Both must exist.

7. Every auto run writes a RUN_SUMMARY.json alongside the log: { run_id, started_at, ended_at, result: pushed|blocked|noop|error, sessions_exported, shards_written, findings: {new, seen, blocked_by}, policy, push: {repo, commit_sha, files} }. Tauri Dashboard reads latest RUN_SUMMARY.json for 'last run' card.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: DataClaw — Auto runs, privacy-filter PII, sharded HF uploads, folder rules, Tauri menubar app (v4)

## Overview

DataClaw today is a Python CLI that discovers coding-agent sessions (`claude`/`codex`/`gemini`/`opencode`/`openclaw`/`kimi`/`custom`), anonymizes paths & usernames (`dataclaw/anonymizer.py`), runs regex + entropy redaction (`dataclaw/secrets.py`), writes a single flat `dataclaw_conversations.jsonl`, and pushes to one HF dataset repo (`dataclaw/cli.py:409–456`).

This plan delivers: (1) `dataclaw auto` replacing the human confirm gate; (2) `openai/privacy-filter` PII augmentation; (3) sharded HF uploads with per-session-id dedup + HF-hub merge; (4) folder rules / buckets; (5) a **fully self-contained Tauri menubar app** signed + notarized + auto-updating, with macOS Keychain HF auth, structured logging, push retry + failed-run persistence, and one-click rollback.

## User decisions (genuinely open — defaults chosen so work proceeds; both overridable without architectural rework)

**1. Auto-mode default policy** — implementation defaults to `strict` (block push on any new privacy-filter finding); user can flip to `permissive` (auto-redact + push + surface after) via `dataclaw enable-auto --policy permissive`. Flipping only changes the default value read at enable-time; no code rework. Recommendation: **strict** (publishing is irreversible).

**2. Folder-rules specification style** — implementation defaults to explicit `folder_rules.buckets = {name: [project_key, ...]}` as the primary form, with optional tag fallback via `project_tags` + `buckets_by_tag`. User can flip to tag-only by leaving `buckets` empty and populating `buckets_by_tag`. No architectural rework. Recommendation: **explicit-first**.

## Hard constraints (back-compat + correctness invariants)

- **Legacy function signatures preserved exactly** (`export_to_jsonl`, `push_to_huggingface`).
- **Sharded exports never overwrite prior sessions**: per-session-id dedup on write + HF-hub-aware merge that explicitly handles `RepositoryNotFoundError`, `EntryNotFoundError`, `LocalEntryNotFoundError` as "no prior file, continue"; other network errors abort the run.
- **Uploads are manifest-scoped** via exact `allow_patterns` (not globs).
- **Per-run staging dirs** (`~/.dataclaw/export/runs/{uuid4}/`); failed-push runs preserved separately at `~/.dataclaw/staging/{run_id}/` for `--retry-only`.
- **`last_export_cutoff` advances only on push success**.
- **Privacy-filter is opt-in**; base install never imports `torch`/`transformers`.
- **`confirm` accepts a positional `path` argument** (dir or file); `--file/-f` preserved as alias.
- **.dmg is fully self-contained**: PyInstaller `universal2` sidecar bundled into the .app; no `pip install` required.
- **HF token lives in macOS Keychain**, not in config.json or env files. Scheduler does not carry `HF_TOKEN`; the scheduled CLI reads from Keychain at runtime.
- **Structured JSON logs** daily-rotated at `~/.dataclaw/logs/auto-YYYY-MM-DD.jsonl`; no log line contains the HF token.
- **`chmod 0600`** on config.json, plist, systemd unit, and staging manifests.
- **Signed + notarized + stapled .dmg** on GitHub Releases; unsigned build for PR CI; `tauri-plugin-updater` with signed `latest.json`.
- **Rollback**: `dataclaw rollback --commit <sha>` + Tauri revert button.

## Settled decisions (locked from prior iterations — excluding the two user-open questions above)

Per-run staging + manifest-scoped upload (SD-004/SD-006). Same-day merge via hf_hub_download + session-id dedup + exception taxonomy covers RepoNotFound/EntryNotFound/LocalEntryNotFound (SD-005). `confirm` directory-aware via manifest with positional path arg + `--file` alias (SD-007). Pipeline-simple decoder with contract test gate (SD-008). `device`/`min_score` keyword-only throughout privacy_filter (SD-009). Scheduler env allowlist drops HF_TOKEN (updated); `WorkingDirectory=$HOME`; `shutil.which` for binary; plist/config/units `chmod 0600` (SD-010). Auto exit codes 0/2/3/4 (SD-011). Tauri thin GUI + CLI set/remove/show-secrets verbs (SD-012). Cooled post-parse filter + aware-datetime cutoff (SD-013). Base-install torch/transformers isolation + `transformers>=4.57` floor with preflight (SD-014). Bucket changes prospective-only; rebucket deferred to v2 (SD-015). **New: SD-016 PyInstaller universal2 sidecar; SD-017 Keychain HF token; SD-018 structured logs + RUN_SUMMARY; SD-019 push retry + persistent staging + `--retry-only`; SD-020 Dev-ID signed/notarized + release.yml; SD-021 tauri-plugin-updater with signed latest.json; SD-022 `dataclaw rollback` + Tauri revert.**

## Phase 2: Sharded export — per-run staging + dedup + manifest-scoped upload

### Step 6: Path resolver + `export_to_shards` (`dataclaw/cli.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `_resolve_shard_path(root, session, config)` and `_bucket_for_project(session, config)` (Step 8).
2. **Add** `export_to_shards(selected_projects, run_dir, anonymizer, config, *, include_thinking=True, custom_strings=None, cooled_only=False, since=None, fetch_existing=True) -> dict`:
   - Pre-flight: compute target shard paths.
   - If `fetch_existing and config.get("repo")`: for each target path, `hf_hub_download(repo_id, path_in_repo, local_dir=run_dir, local_dir_use_symlinks=False)`. **Exception taxonomy**:
     ```python
     from huggingface_hub.utils import (
         RepositoryNotFoundError, EntryNotFoundError, LocalEntryNotFoundError,
     )
     try:
         hf_hub_download(...)
     except (RepositoryNotFoundError, EntryNotFoundError, LocalEntryNotFoundError):
         merge_source[path] = "new"   # first-time push for this path; no merge needed
     except HfHubHTTPError as e:
         if e.response is not None and e.response.status_code == 404:
             merge_source[path] = "new"
         else:
             raise  # abort run on genuine network errors
     ```
   - Load existing `session_id`s per target path into `seen_ids`; open shard in append mode.
   - Second pass: skip sessions already in `seen_ids[path]`; else write.
   - Strip internal `_source_file`/`_project_dir_name` before JSON write.
   - Write manifest atomically (Step 7) with `chmod 0600` on the manifest file.
3. **Do not touch** `export_to_jsonl`.

## Phase 7: Self-contained .app — PyInstaller sidecar

### Step 30: PyInstaller spec + build script (`pyinstaller.spec`, `Makefile`, `scripts/build-sidecar.sh`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `pyinstaller.spec` pinning `--name dataclaw --onefile --target-arch universal2` with explicit hidden-imports (`huggingface_hub`, `dataclaw.parser`, `dataclaw.secrets`, `dataclaw.anonymizer`, `dataclaw.privacy_filter`, `dataclaw.logging`, `dataclaw.auth`, `dataclaw.scheduler`) and `collect_data("huggingface_hub")`.
2. **`scripts/build-sidecar.sh`**:
   ```bash
   #!/usr/bin/env bash
   set -euo pipefail
   ARCH="${1:-$(uname -m)}"   # arm64 or x86_64
   uv run pyinstaller pyinstaller.spec --clean --noconfirm
   # Tauri externalBin naming convention: <name>-<target-triple>
   case "$ARCH" in
     arm64|aarch64) TRIPLE="aarch64-apple-darwin" ;;
     x86_64)        TRIPLE="x86_64-apple-darwin" ;;
   esac
   mkdir -p app/src-tauri/binaries
   cp dist/dataclaw "app/src-tauri/binaries/dataclaw-${TRIPLE}"
   codesign --remove-signature "app/src-tauri/binaries/dataclaw-${TRIPLE}" || true  # Tauri re-signs
   ```
3. **`Makefile`** targets:
   ```makefile
   build-sidecar:        ; scripts/build-sidecar.sh
   build-sidecar-arm64:  ; scripts/build-sidecar.sh arm64
   build-sidecar-x86_64: ; scripts/build-sidecar.sh x86_64
   ```
4. Add `pyinstaller` to the `dev` extra.

### Step 31: Tauri externalBin declaration (`app/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add**:
   ```json
   {"bundle": {"externalBin": ["binaries/dataclaw"]}}
   ```
   Tauri looks up `binaries/dataclaw-<target-triple>` at bundle time and copies it into `.app/Contents/MacOS/`, signing it alongside the app.
2. **Update** `dataclaw.rs` to use `Command::new_sidecar("dataclaw")` as **first** resolution step (revise Step 27 order).

   jobs:
     build-app:
       strategy: { matrix: { os: [macos-14, macos-13] } }
       runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
       steps:
         - uses: actions/checkout@v4
         - uses: actions/setup-python@v5 { with: { python-version: '3.12' } }
         - uses: astral-sh/setup-uv@v5
         - run: uv sync --extra dev --extra pii

         - uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4 { with: { version: 9 } }
         - run: pnpm -C app install --frozen-lockfile
         - env: { DATACLAW_SIGNING_IDENTITY: "" }   # unsigned on PR CI
           run: pnpm -C app tauri build

## Phase 8: Structured logging + `RUN_SUMMARY.json` + Logs UI

### Step 35: `dataclaw/logging.py` — JSON-lines rotating logger (new module)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **`setup_logging(run_id, level="INFO") -> logging.Logger`**:
   ```python
   import logging, json, sys
   from pathlib import Path
   from logging.handlers import TimedRotatingFileHandler
   LOG_DIR = Path.home() / ".dataclaw" / "logs"
   class JsonFormatter(logging.Formatter):
       def format(self, record):
           payload = {"ts": self.formatTime(record), "level": record.levelname,
                      "logger": record.name, "msg": record.getMessage(),
                      "run_id": getattr(record, "run_id", None),
                      "phase": getattr(record, "phase", None),
                      "session_id": getattr(record, "session_id", None),
                      "project": getattr(record, "project", None),
                      "source": getattr(record, "source", None),
                      "extra": getattr(record, "extra_data", None)}
           if record.exc_info: payload["exc"] = self.formatException(record.exc_info)
           return json.dumps({k:v for k,v in payload.items() if v is not None})
   def setup_logging(run_id, level="INFO"):
       LOG_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
       handler = TimedRotatingFileHandler(LOG_DIR / "auto.jsonl",
                                          when="midnight", backupCount=30, utc=True)
       handler.suffix = "%Y-%m-%d"; handler.setFormatter(JsonFormatter())
       stderr_handler = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stderr); stderr_handler.setLevel("WARNING")
       stderr_handler.setFormatter(JsonFormatter())
       logger = logging.getLogger("dataclaw")
       logger.setLevel(level); logger.handlers = [handler, stderr_handler]
       return logging.LoggerAdapter(logger, {"run_id": run_id})
   ```
2. **Rotation**: `TimedRotatingFileHandler` with `when="midnight"` produces `auto.jsonl.2026-04-25` etc; `backupCount=30` auto-prunes.
3. **Token scrubbing**: `JsonFormatter` runs `msg` through `_scrub_secrets(text)` which `re.sub`s any HF_TOKEN-shaped pattern (`hf_[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}`) to `[REDACTED]`. Applied to all payload fields.

### Step 36: Wire logging into every phase (`dataclaw/cli.py`, `dataclaw/parser.py`, `dataclaw/privacy_filter.py`, `dataclaw/scheduler.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Emit `phase_start(phase=…, inputs=…)`, `phase_end(phase=…, outputs=…, duration_ms=…, redactions_applied=…)` at boundaries:
   - Discovery, parse, redact, privacy_filter, push, confirm, schedule.
2. Per-session `session_parsed(msg_count, tokens, cooled_at)`.
3. Per-finding `pf_finding(fingerprint, category, confidence, known)`.
4. Per upload `hf_upload_start/end(file, shard_path, repo, commit_sha)`.
5. All exceptions via `logger.exception("...", extra={"phase": ...})`.

## Phase 9: macOS Keychain HF token + `dataclaw hf {login,logout,whoami}` + sidecar token delivery

### Step 41: `_resolve_hf_token()` helper (`dataclaw/auth.py` — new)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **New module**:
   ```python
   import os
   from pathlib import Path
   KEYRING_SERVICE = "io.dataclaw.app"
   KEYRING_ACCOUNT = "hf_token"

   def _resolve_hf_token() -> str | None:
       """Order: env > macOS Keychain (via keyring) > huggingface_hub default path."""
       tok = os.environ.get("HF_TOKEN")
       if tok: return tok
       try:
           import keyring
           tok = keyring.get_password(KEYRING_SERVICE, KEYRING_ACCOUNT)
           if tok: return tok
       except ImportError:
           pass
       p = Path.home() / ".cache" / "huggingface" / "token"
       if p.exists():
           try: return p.read_text().strip() or None
           except OSError: return None
       return None

   def _store_hf_token(tok: str) -> None:
       import keyring
       keyring.set_password(KEYRING_SERVICE, KEYRING_ACCOUNT, tok)

   def _delete_hf_token() -> None:
       try:
           import keyring; keyring.delete_password(KEYRING_SERVICE, KEYRING_ACCOUNT)
       except Exception: pass
   ```
2. **Wire** `push_to_huggingface` and `push_shards_to_huggingface` to set `os.environ["HF_TOKEN"] = _resolve_hf_token()` (if found) before `HfApi()` calls — `huggingface_hub` picks it up natively.
3. **On missing token during `auto`**: exit 2 with `fix: "Launch DataClaw.app and sign in, OR run: dataclaw hf login --token-stdin"`.

### Step 42: `dataclaw hf {login,logout,whoami}` CLI verbs (`dataclaw/cli.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Add `hf` subparser with `login [--token-stdin]`, `logout`, `whoami`.
2. Handlers:
   - `login`: read one line from stdin (or prompt via `getpass`); `_store_hf_token(tok)`; call `HfApi().whoami()` to verify.
   - `logout`: `_delete_hf_token()`.
   - `whoami`: print `HfApi([REDACTED]()).whoami()`.

### Step 43: Tauri first-run token modal + Keychain writes (`app/src-tauri/src/hf.rs`, `app/src/routes/Auth.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Rust** `hf.rs` uses `keyring = "3"` crate:
   ```rust
   use keyring::Entry;
   const SERVICE: &str = "io.dataclaw.app";
   const ACCOUNT: &str = "hf_token";

   #[tauri::command]
   async fn hf_save_token(token: String) -> Result<(), String> {
       Entry::new(SERVICE, ACCOUNT).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
           .set_password(&token).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
   }
   #[tauri::command]
   async fn hf_load_token() -> Result<Option<String>, String> {
       match Entry::new(SERVICE, ACCOUNT).and_then(|e| e.get_password()) {
           Ok(t) => Ok(Some(t)),
           Err(keyring::Error::NoEntry) => Ok(None),
           Err(e) => Err(e.to_string()),
       }
   }
   #[tauri::command]
   async fn hf_delete_token() -> Result<(), String> { ... }
   #[tauri::command]
   async fn hf_whoami() -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> {
       run_cli_with_token(&["hf", "whoami"]).await
   }
   ```
2. **Sidecar token delivery**: `run_cli_with_token` loads the token from Keychain and passes it via environment on the spawned child:
   ```rust
   async fn run_cli_with_token(args: &[&str]) -> Result<Value, String> {
       let token = hf_load_token().await?.unwrap_or_default();
       let mut cmd = tauri::api::process::Command::new_sidecar("dataclaw")?;
       if !token.is_empty() { cmd = cmd.env("HF_TOKEN", &token); }
       let out = cmd.args(args).output().await?;
       parse_json_block(&out.stdout)
   }
   ```
3. **First-run modal** (`Auth.tsx`): blocking modal on startup when `hf_load_token()` returns `None` and `config.repo` is set. Fields: token input (password, show/hide toggle), link to `https://huggingface.co/settings/tokens`, "Test" button calls `hf_whoami`, "Skip — I'll add it later" (puts app in read-only; Run Now disabled with tooltip).
4. **Config tab** gets an "HF Account" section: status line, Test button, Clear token button.
5. **Allowlist** in `tauri.conf.json`: `hf_save_token`, `hf_load_token`, `hf_delete_token`, `hf_whoami`.

### Step 44: Scheduler/CLI token resolution (cross-reference from Phase 5)
**Scope:** Small
1. **No HF_TOKEN in plist/systemd env.** Scheduled `dataclaw auto` runs as the user's login session in `gui/$(id -u)` launchd domain; `_resolve_hf_token()` reads Keychain successfully when user is logged in. If Keychain access is denied (unlikely under gui-domain), fall back to `~/.cache/huggingface/token`, then fail with the install-hint from Step 41.
2. **Document** in `docs/AUTO_MODE.md` (short section): scheduled auto runs require the user to be logged in; the job is skipped (waits for next window) when user is logged out.

## Phase 10: Push retry + exponential backoff + persistent failed-run staging

### Step 46: Retry wrapper around `push_shards_to_huggingface` (`dataclaw/cli.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **New helper `_push_with_retry(run_dir, repo_id, manifest, logger)`**:
   ```python
   import time, random
   BACKOFF_SECONDS = [30, 120, 600]   # 30s, 2m, 10m
   RETRIABLE = (ConnectionError, TimeoutError)   # + urllib3.exceptions.*

   def _is_retriable(exc) -> bool:
       from huggingface_hub.utils import HfHubHTTPError
       if isinstance(exc, RETRIABLE): return True
       if isinstance(exc, HfHubHTTPError):
           sc = exc.response.status_code if exc.response else 0
           if sc in (401, 403, 400): return False  # auth / bad request — fail fast
           if sc == 429 or 500 <= sc < 600: return True
       return False

   def _retry_after_seconds(exc, attempt: int) -> float:
       # Honor Retry-After header on 429
       try:
           retry = exc.response.headers.get("Retry-After")
           if retry: return float(retry)
       except Exception: pass
       return BACKOFF_SECONDS[min(attempt, len(BACKOFF_SECONDS) - 1)] + random.uniform(0, 5)

   def _push_with_retry(run_dir, repo_id, manifest, logger):
       total_wait = 0.0
       last_exc = None
       for attempt in range(3):
           try:
               logger.info("hf_upload_start", extra={"phase":"push","attempt":attempt})
               url = push_shards_to_huggingface(run_dir, repo_id, manifest)
               logger.info("hf_upload_end", extra={"phase":"push","attempt":attempt,"url":url})
               return url, attempt + 1, total_wait
           except Exception as exc:
               last_exc = exc
               if not _is_retriable(exc) or attempt == 2:
                   logger.exception("hf_upload_failed", extra={"phase":"push","attempt":attempt})
                   break
               wait = _retry_after_seconds(exc, attempt)
               logger.warning("hf_upload_retry", extra={"wait": wait, "attempt": attempt})
               time.sleep(wait); total_wait += wait
       raise PushFailed(last_exc, attempts=3, backoff_seconds_total=total_wait)
   ```
2. **`auto` handler**: on `PushFailed`, **move the run dir** from `~/.dataclaw/export/runs/{run_id}` → `~/.dataclaw/staging/{run_id}` (preserved for `--retry-only`), record `last_auto_run.result="error"` + `push_attempts` + `backoff_seconds_total`, exit code 4.

### Step 47: `dataclaw auto --retry-only` (`dataclaw/cli.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Subparser flag `--retry-only` skips parsing and targets the most recent failed staging dir at `~/.dataclaw/staging/`. Reads its manifest, invokes `_push_with_retry` directly. On success, moves the dir to `~/.dataclaw/staging/published/{run_id}/` (or deletes, per `config.auto.keep_published_staging: bool = false`).

### Step 48: Staging-size warning + `dataclaw clean-staging` (`dataclaw/cli.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. On every `auto` invocation, compute `du` over `~/.dataclaw/staging/`; if > 5 GB, `logger.warning("staging_size_exceeded", extra={"bytes": ...})`. Tauri Dashboard reads `RUN_SUMMARY.json.warnings` and surfaces the alert.
2. New subcommand `dataclaw clean-staging [--yes]` — `shutil.rmtree` under `~/.dataclaw/staging/` (excluding `published/`). Requires confirmation unless `--yes`.

### Step 49: Tauri retry & clear buttons (`app/src/routes/Dashboard.tsx`)
**Scope:** Small
1. When `last_auto_run.result == "error"`, show a red "Last push failed — Retry" button → invokes `dataclaw_auto_retry_only`. Show attempts + total backoff from `RUN_SUMMARY.json`.
2. "Clear staging" button in Config → invokes `dataclaw_clean_staging({yes: true})`.

### Step 50: Tests — retry + persistence (`tests/test_push_retry.py` — new)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **`test_push_retry_backoff`** using `freezegun` + mocked `HfApi.upload_folder`: raise `ConnectionError` attempts 1–2, succeed on 3; assert 3 attempts and total wait ≈ 150s (30+120).
2. `test_push_429_honors_retry_after` — mock 429 with `Retry-After: 60` header; assert wait ≈ 60s.
3. **`test_push_401_fails_immediately`** — no retries on auth errors.
4. `test_push_exhaustion_exits_4_and_preserves_staging` — all 3 attempts fail; run dir moved to `~/.dataclaw/staging/{run_id}`; `last_auto_run.result="error"`; `config.last_auto_run.push_attempts == 3`.
5. **`test_retry_only_reuses_failed_run_dir`** — seed a failed staging dir; `auto --retry-only` finds it, does not re-parse, pushes successfully; dir moves to `published/`.
6. `test_staging_size_warning_above_5gb` — mock `shutil.disk_usage` / directory walk.

## Phase 11: Code signing + notarization + updater + release infrastructure

### Step 51: `docs/RELEASING.md` (new)
**Scope:** Small
1. Document one-time manual steps: (a) enroll at developer.apple.com/programs/enroll; (b) generate Developer ID Application cert via Xcode or CLI; export as `.p12`; (c) generate App Store Connect API Key (`.p8`) under Users & Access → Integrations; note Issuer ID + Key ID + Team ID; (d) `pnpm -C app tauri signer generate -w ~/.tauri/dataclaw-updater.key` — store private key as `TAURI_UPDATER_SIGNING_KEY` GitHub secret + password manager; public key committed to `tauri.conf.json` (Step 53).
2. List required GitHub repo secrets: `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64`, `APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD`, `APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64`, `APPLE_API_KEY_ID`, `APPLE_API_ISSUER`, `APPLE_TEAM_ID`, `APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY`, `TAURI_UPDATER_SIGNING_KEY`, `TAURI_UPDATER_SIGNING_KEY_PASSWORD`.

### Step 52: Entitlements (`app/src-tauri/entitlements.plist`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** minimal hardened-runtime entitlements:
   ```xml
   <?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
   <!DOCTYPE plist PUBLIC "-//Apple//DTD PLIST 1.0//EN"
     "http://www.apple.com/DTDs/PropertyList-1.0.dtd">
   <plist version="1.0"><dict>
     <key>com.apple.security.cs.allow-unsigned-executable-memory</key><false/>
     <key>com.apple.security.cs.disable-library-validation</key><false/>
     <key>com.apple.security.network.client</key><true/>
     <key>com.apple.security.files.user-selected.read-write</key><true/>
   </dict></plist>
   ```
2. If PyInstaller runtime requires `disable-library-validation=true`, document WHY in a comment and bump to `<true/>` — only as needed.

**Scope:** Small
1. **Add**:
   ```json
   {
     "bundle": {
       "externalBin": ["binaries/dataclaw"],
       "macOS": {
         "signingIdentity": "${DATACLAW_SIGNING_IDENTITY}",
         "entitlements": "entitlements.plist",
         "hardenedRuntime": true,
         "providerShortName": "${APPLE_TEAM_ID}"
       }
     },

         "active": true,

       }
     }

   ```
   `${...}` values resolved from env at build time. `pubkey` is committed (public); private key stays in secrets.

### Step 54: `tauri-plugin-updater` wiring (`Cargo.toml`, `package.json`, `main.rs`, `App.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium

2. `package.json`: `@tauri-apps/plugin-updater: "^2"`.
3. `main.rs`: register the plugin; expose `check_for_updates` and `install_update` commands; add "Check for updates" menu item to tray.
4. Frontend: on launch + once/week (driven by `config.updater.last_check`), call `check_for_updates`; on update available, show non-blocking toast "Update available: vX.Y.Z — click to install". Only proceeds on user click; on click, `install_update` downloads + verifies ed25519 signature + relaunches.

### Step 55: `.github/workflows/release.yml` (new)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Triggers on `push: tags: v*`:
   ```yaml
   name: release
   on: { push: { tags: ['v*'] } }
   jobs:
     build:
       strategy: { matrix: { include: [{os: macos-14, triple: aarch64-apple-darwin},
                                       {os: macos-13, triple: x86_64-apple-darwin}] } }
       runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
       steps:
         - uses: actions/checkout@v4
         - uses: actions/setup-python@v5 { with: { python-version: '3.12' } }
         - uses: astral-sh/setup-uv@v5
         - uses: pnpm/action-setup@v4 { with: { version: 9 } }
         - name: Import Developer ID cert
           run: |
             echo "${{ secrets.APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_P12_BASE64 }}" | base64 -d > cert.p12
             security create-keychain -p "" build.keychain
             security default-keychain -s build.keychain
             security unlock-keychain -p "" build.keychain
             security import cert.p12 -k build.keychain -P "${{ secrets.APPLE_DEVELOPER_ID_CERT_PASSWORD }}" -T /usr/bin/codesign
             security set-key-partition-list -S apple-tool:,apple: -s -k "" build.keychain
             rm cert.p12
         - name: Write API key
           run: |
             mkdir -p ~/.appstoreconnect/private_keys
             echo "${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_P8_BASE64 }}" | base64 -d > ~/.appstoreconnect/private_keys/AuthKey_${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}.p8
         - run: uv sync --extra dev --extra pii
         - run: make build-sidecar-arm64   # or x86_64 per matrix triple
         - run: pnpm -C app install --frozen-lockfile
         - name: Build signed + notarized app
           env:
             DATACLAW_SIGNING_IDENTITY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_SIGNING_IDENTITY }}
             APPLE_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_KEY_ID }}
             APPLE_API_ISSUER: ${{ secrets.APPLE_API_ISSUER }}
             APPLE_TEAM_ID: ${{ secrets.APPLE_TEAM_ID }}
             TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY: ${{ secrets.TAURI_UPDATER_SIGNING_KEY }}
             TAURI_SIGNING_PRIVATE_KEY_PASSWORD: ${{ secrets.TAURI_UPDATER_SIGNING_KEY_PASSWORD }}
           run: pnpm -C app tauri build
         - name: Upload to release
           env: { GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }} }
           run: |
             DMG=$(ls app/src-tauri/target/release/bundle/dmg/*.dmg)
             SIG=$(ls app/src-tauri/target/release/bundle/macos/*.app.tar.gz.sig)
             ARCHIVE=$(ls app/src-tauri/target/release/bundle/macos/*.app.tar.gz)
             gh release upload "${GITHUB_REF_NAME}" "$DMG" "$ARCHIVE" "$SIG" --clobber
     publish-latest:
       needs: build
       runs-on: ubuntu-latest
       steps:
         - uses: actions/checkout@v4
         - name: Assemble latest.json
           env: { GH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }} }
           run: |
             # Download all .sig files, build the {platforms: {darwin-aarch64: {url, signature}, darwin-x86_64: {...}}} JSON
             scripts/build-latest-json.sh > latest.json
             gh release upload "${GITHUB_REF_NAME}" latest.json --clobber
   ```
2. **`scripts/build-latest-json.sh`** (new) pulls `.sig` contents + tag-based URLs; emits the Tauri-updater schema.

## Phase 12: Emergency rollback — `dataclaw rollback` + Tauri revert

### Step 60: `dataclaw rollback` CLI verb (`dataclaw/cli.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Subparser**:
   ```python
   rb = sub.add_parser("rollback", help="Revert the dataset repo to a previous commit.")
   group = rb.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)
   group.add_argument("--commit", help="Target commit SHA to revert to")
   group.add_argument("--list", action="store_true", help="Print the last N commits")
   rb.add_argument("--repo", help="Override configured repo")
   rb.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
   rb.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=20)
   ```
2. **Handlers**:
   - `--list`: `HfApi().list_repo_commits(repo_id, repo_type="dataset")` → print JSON `[{sha, message, author, created_at, files_changed}]` (files via `list_repo_files` diff between consecutive commits — cached).
   - `--commit SHA`: compute the file tree at that commit via `list_repo_files(repo_id, revision=SHA, repo_type="dataset")` → build `CommitOperation` list where every current file is replaced with the version from the target revision (`CommitOperationAdd(path_or_fileobj=hf_hub_download(...revision=SHA), path_in_repo=...)`); files present NOW but absent at target revision become `CommitOperationDelete(path)`. Commit with message `Rollback to {SHA[:8]}: {original_message}`.
   - `--dry-run`: prints the planned operations without calling `create_commit`.
3. **Structured log event**:
   ```python
   logger.info("rollback", extra={"phase":"rollback", "initiator":"cli",
                                  "target_commit": sha, "previous_commit": head_sha,
                                  "reverted_files": paths, "dry_run": args.dry_run})
   ```

### Step 61: Tauri "Recent pushes" UI + revert button (`app/src/routes/Releases.tsx`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Tab "Releases": calls `dataclaw_rollback_list`; renders a list with timestamp, message, files-changed count; red "Revert" button per row.
2. On click: confirm modal with commit details + file count; on confirm → `dataclaw_rollback_commit(sha)`. On success: banner "Rolled back {timestamp}" until dismissed.
3. Rust commands `dataclaw_rollback_list()` / `dataclaw_rollback_commit(sha)` in `dataclaw.rs`.

### Step 62: `README.md` — Privacy & Redaction section
**Scope:** Small
1. Add: "If something slips through, DataClaw has a one-click rollback. You can revert your HF dataset to any previous commit from the app or via `dataclaw rollback --commit <sha>`."

### Step 63: Tests — rollback (`tests/test_rollback.py` — new)
**Scope:** Small
1. **`test_rollback_list_shows_recent_commits`** — stub `HfApi.list_repo_commits`; assert JSON output shape.
2. **`test_rollback_reverts_to_commit`** — push two synthetic commits to a stub repo; call `rollback --commit <first_sha>`; verify `HfApi.create_commit` called with the expected `CommitOperation` list; verify `list_repo_files(revision=first_sha)` hash matches post-rollback `list_repo_files(revision=HEAD)`.
3. **`test_rollback_dry_run_does_not_call_create_commit`**.
4. **`test_rollback_logs_structured_event`** — assert log line contains `phase=rollback`, `target_commit`, `previous_commit`, `reverted_files`.

---

## Execution Order

1. **Phase 1** — substrate (schema, deps, cooled filter, ISO parsing, chmod 0600 on config).
2. **Phase 2** — sharded export with exception taxonomy + positional `confirm` arg; validate same-day merge end-to-end.
3. **Phase 3** — bucket resolver + config CLI verbs + copy sync.
4. **Phase 4** — privacy-filter behind `pii` extra; run Step 21 preflight before merge.
5. **Phase 5** — auto + scheduler (HF_TOKEN removed from allowlist).
6. **Phase 7** — PyInstaller sidecar (before Phase 6 frontend work so sidecar-first resolution is live during Tauri dev).
7. **Phase 6** — Tauri base scaffolding.
8. **Phase 8** — structured logging (wires through all prior phases).
9. **Phase 9** — HF Keychain auth + `hf` verbs + Tauri first-run modal.
10. **Phase 10** — push retry + persistent failed-run staging + `--retry-only`.
11. **Phase 11** — signing + notarization + updater + release.yml + docs/RELEASING.md.
12. **Phase 12** — rollback (CLI + Tauri).

Phases 7–12 can be parallelized by an implementation team; dependency edges: 7 → 6 (sidecar before Tauri UI beyond scaffolding); 9 → 5 (Keychain replaces token in scheduler env); 11 → 7 + 9 (signing needs sidecar + Keychain code present).

## Validation Order

1. **Unit tests per phase** — `pytest tests/` (adds `test_import_isolation`, `test_privacy_filter`, `test_scheduler`, `test_logging`, `test_auth`, `test_push_retry`, `test_rollback`).
2. **Legacy parity** — `test_export_to_jsonl_flat_path_unchanged_byte_for_byte`, `test_push_to_huggingface_legacy_signature_unchanged`, `test_confirm_file_flag_still_accepted_as_alias`.
3. **Same-day merge smoke** — two sharded runs 5 min apart; shard contains all sessions, no duplicates.
4. **Staging retry smoke** — kill network mid-push; verify `~/.dataclaw/staging/{run_id}/` preserved; `dataclaw auto --retry-only` resumes.
5. **Token-scrubbing** — `test_token_never_written_to_config_json` + `test_token_scrubbed_from_logs`.
6. **Contract test** — `pytest -m pii tests/test_privacy_filter.py::test_pipeline_decoder_matches_model_card_examples` with `dataclaw[pii]` installed.
7. **CI matrix** — `ci.yml` passes on macos-14 + macos-13 PR builds; unsigned `.app` + `.dmg` present.
8. **Release dry-run** (manual `info`): tag `v0.0.0-rc1` on a fork → `release.yml` runs → Dev-ID signed + notarized `.dmg` on draft release → `spctl --assess` reports "Notarized Developer ID" → install on clean Mac → `tauri-plugin-updater` sees no newer release (negative test).
9. **Rollback smoke** (manual `info`): push two commits → `dataclaw rollback --commit <first>` → verify HF dataset tree matches first commit.
10. **Tauri E2E (manual `info`)**: fresh Mac, no pip install → open .dmg → first-run HF token modal → Run Now → Logs tab streams → blocked strict-mode finding → Findings tab "Add to redact list" → Run Now retries → Releases tab shows new commit → Revert → rolled-back banner.

## Risk & tradeoff notes

- **Keychain in launchd gui-domain**: works when user is logged in. If user is logged out, auto-run fails with a clear exit-2 error; next run at the scheduled time re-attempts. Documented in Phase 9 Step 44.
- **PyInstaller universal2 sidecar**: ~40–80 MB per arch; `.dmg` ~120 MB total. Acceptable for a menubar app.
- **Notarization roundtrip**: adds ~5–15 min to `release.yml` runtime per arch. Tauri bundler handles the polling.
- **`tauri-plugin-updater` signing**: ed25519 private key must be rotated if leaked. Rotation involves publishing a new public key in a plan-documented app release, then the next release uses it. Users on older versions won't accept the new key — document as a known limitation.
- **PushFailed preservation**: the failed-run dir is NOT garbage-collected automatically (separate from `_cleanup_stale_runs(keep=3)` on the normal runs dir). User must `dataclaw clean-staging` or retry.
- **Rollback scope**: reverts the HF repo tree only; local `last_export_cutoff` is NOT auto-rewound, so sessions present in the pre-rollback revision won't be re-pushed. Plan for v2: `dataclaw rollback --reset-cutoff`. Document as `info`-level TODO.
- **Entitlements minimalism**: `disable-library-validation=false` by default; bump only if PyInstaller runtime fails at launch. Notarization works either way.

    "Transformers version floor: plan tentatively pins `>=4.57`. Phase 4 preflight verifies. If you know the canonical release (4.56 / 4.58), confirm.",
    "Entitlements: plan uses minimal hardened-runtime entitlements with `disable-library-validation=false`. If PyInstaller runtime fails at launch due to dynamic library loading, we bump to `<true/>` and document. Acceptable?",
    "Updater signing key rotation: plan documents that a compromised ed25519 key requires a new release with a new public key; users on older versions won't accept the new key. Acceptable as a known limitation?",

  "assumptions": [
    "PyInstaller `--onefile --target-arch universal2` produces a single binary that runs on both arm64 and x86_64 Macs. CI matrix builds once per arch (macos-14 for arm64, macos-13 for x86_64) to produce `dataclaw-aarch64-apple-darwin` and `dataclaw-x86_64-apple-darwin` Tauri externalBin sidecars.",
    "Tauri's externalBin naming convention auto-resolves at bundle time by appending the host's target triple; the sidecar lands at `.app/Contents/MacOS/dataclaw-<triple>` and is signed alongside the .app.",
    "Structured logging uses `logging.handlers.TimedRotatingFileHandler` with `when='midnight'` and `backupCount=30`. Token-scrubbing via `re.sub(r'hf_[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}', '[REDACTED]', msg)` applied inside `JsonFormatter.format`.",
    "`_resolve_hf_token()` priority: env `HF_TOKEN` > macOS Keychain via `keyring` > `~/.cache/huggingface/token` (huggingface_hub legacy). Scheduler env allowlist drops HF_TOKEN. Launchd gui-domain Keychain access works when user is logged in; auto run exits with code 2 + fix-it hint when token is unreachable.",
    "Push retry uses exponential backoff with delays `[30, 120, 600]` seconds + `random.uniform(0, 5)` jitter. Retriable: `ConnectionError`, `TimeoutError`, `urllib3` transport exceptions, `HfHubHTTPError` with status 429 (honors `Retry-After` header) or 5xx. Non-retriable: 400/401/403 (auth/bad request) fail immediately. Exit code 4 on exhaustion.",
    "Failed auto runs move their run dir from `~/.dataclaw/export/runs/{run_id}/` to `~/.dataclaw/staging/{run_id}/` for preservation. `dataclaw auto --retry-only` targets the most recent `staging/` dir, reads its manifest, and invokes `_push_with_retry` without re-parsing sessions.",
    "Same-day merge exception taxonomy: `RepositoryNotFoundError`, `EntryNotFoundError`, `LocalEntryNotFoundError`, and `HfHubHTTPError` with `status_code == 404` are all treated as 'no prior file, continue as first-time push'. Any other exception (network errors, non-404 HTTP errors) raises and aborts the run.",
    "`dataclaw confirm` gets a new positional `path` argument with `nargs='?'`; the existing `--file/-f` flag is preserved as a deprecated alias via `dest='legacy_file'`. Handler uses `args.path or args.legacy_file`.",
    "macOS signing/notarization uses Tauri's built-in bundler via env vars `DATACLAW_SIGNING_IDENTITY`, `APPLE_API_KEY`, `APPLE_API_KEY_ID`, `APPLE_API_ISSUER`, `APPLE_TEAM_ID`. Tauri handles codesign + notarytool submit + stapler automatically when those are set.",
    "`tauri-plugin-updater = '2'` with ed25519 signatures; public key committed to `tauri.conf.json`, private key in `TAURI_UPDATER_SIGNING_KEY` GitHub secret. `latest.json` assembled by `scripts/build-latest-json.sh` in a separate `publish-latest` job after all arch builds complete. Updater checks on launch + weekly via `config.updater.last_check`. No auto-install without user click.",
    "`dataclaw rollback --commit <sha>` uses `HfApi.list_repo_files(repo_id, revision=sha)` + `hf_hub_download(revision=sha)` per-file to reconstruct the target tree, then `HfApi.create_commit` with `CommitOperationAdd`/`CommitOperationDelete` operations. `--dry-run` prints planned operations. `--list` uses `HfApi.list_repo_commits` and prints JSON for Tauri consumption.",
    "`chmod 0600` applied to `~/.dataclaw/config.json`, `.dataclaw/manifest.json`, `~/Library/LaunchAgents/io.dataclaw.auto.plist`, and `~/.config/systemd/user/dataclaw-auto.{service,timer}`. Ensures only the owner can read files that might transiently contain sensitive data.",
    "Auto-mode default policy (strict) and folder-rules style (explicit-first) are implementation defaults the user can flip via CLI flags without architectural rework. Removed from 'Settled decisions' list; listed only in 'User decisions (genuinely open)' section with explicit overridability framing.",
    "README receives five new sections: app-install (Phase 7), auto-mode (Phase 5), privacy filter (Phase 4), buckets (Phase 3), rollback privacy (Phase 12). `docs/RELEASING.md` is a new maintainer-focused file covering Apple enrollment, cert generation, API key management, and updater key management. `docs/AUTO_MODE.md` is a new user-focused file noting that scheduled auto runs require the user to be logged in (Keychain access requirement).",
    "Phase 7 ships before Phase 6 completes so sidecar-first binary resolution is live during Tauri dev. Phase 9 (Keychain) updates Phase 5 scheduler behavior (drops HF_TOKEN from allowlist). Phase 11 (signing) depends on both Phase 7 (sidecar to sign) and Phase 9 (Keychain code to include in bundle)."
  ],
  "delta_from_previous_percent": 84.87,
  "structure_warnings": []
}

      "id": "DEBT-003",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: inspected `../runpod-lifecycle/src/runpod_lifecycle/pod.py` and `../runpod-lifecycle/src/runpod_lifecycle/ssh.py`. the existing `_build_ssh_client()` helper returns the package's own `runpod_lifecycle.ssh.sshclient` wrapper, not a raw `paramiko.sshclient`. that means step 0's promise that `pod.open_ssh_client() -> paramiko.sshclient` is a 3-line public wrapper around existing logic is not technically accurate, and step 20's `scp.scpclient(ssh.get_transport())` call would not work against the current wrapper type as written.",

      "plan_ids": [
        "build-ip-adapter-for-text-a-20260423-2233"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-004",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked `runpodconfig.from_env()` in `../runpod-lifecycle/src/runpod_lifecycle/config.py`. it parses `runpod_ssh_private_key`, `runpod_ssh_private_key_path`, and related key vars, but it does not consume `runpod_ssh_password`; the current password path comes from pod ssh details inside `_build_ssh_client()`. the fallback clause therefore misstates the existing config surface when it says it reads the same env vars that `runpodconfig.from_env()` already consumes.",
